SCCW — Pay-per-view

Snakeeyes 2008, 30 March 2008

30 March 2008
Content noticeA period document, served unedited. It carries the humor, language, and content typical of its era and community — some of it crude, dated, or offensive, including material that wouldn't be written today. See Curation.
Snakeeyes 2008 Miramar Marine Corps Air Station — San Diego, California ---- Corvus (AWC LiveWire Champion) vs Joaquin Pierre (SCCW Gateway Champion) This is a march of war The last chance to settle the score The battle lines well defined A fight we have both fought before The screen lights up, revealing the images of Joaquin Pierre and Corvus, then cutting out to reveal Corvus nailing the Chapter 13 on Pierre at FUSE on ESPN. Then, two weeks ago, Pierre dropping Corvus with the Au Revoir. It then cuts to Blood Angel, dropping Mr. Hawke to the canvas with the Driver DDT. Hunter Sabuani cheating his way to victory. Mayhem spearing Alan Branch through the stripper pole in STD's office. Bishop planting Cyrus Raynes with the Strain Theory. What do you want from me? You couldn't give me what I need I said this a thousand times before Now watch me walk out that door A clip of those who have recently left, and their final appearances. El Diablo falling prey to the Au Revoir, Gideon Fontaine being pinned by Aimz. I'll never be the person you want me to be What do you want from me? What do you want from me? I'll never see the person you always seem to see What do you need from me? I'll never be the person you want me to be What do you want from me? What do you want from me? I'll never be the person you want me to be. A closeup of Jonathan Rhine brings a cheer from the crowd. This is the march of war A fight that we have fought before You can't make a square peg fit In a hole made of solid brick Jonathan Rhine watching intently a television monitor as Aimz catches Gideon Fontaine in the Rhine Rewind. What do you need from me? I'll never be the person you want me to be The crowd's cheers for Rhine immediately turn into intense booing as Amy Campbell brings her steel chair crashing down on their hero, his flesh and bone no match for the unrelenting metal. Again and again, again and again, until the image on the screen explodes and cuts off to the voices of SCCW, Dave Gibson and the one and only Eugene Ware. Gibson: Ladies and gentlemen welcome to SCCW Snake Eyes! Ware: Tonight, we get to see Amy Campbell beat Jonathan Rhine for the Universal Title, and Lindsey Troy crush his PTC Unified Title hopes! Gibson: That remains to be seen, Eugene. Beating Rhine, as we have seen in the past, is no easy task, and if either Aimz or Troy takes him lightly, they will find themselves ex champions. Ware: I wish I was getting beating up by Aimz right now. By beat up, I mean... Gibson: We all know what you mean, Eugene. In other action tonight on this, the biggest card in recent memory, Joaquin Pierre puts his Gateway Championship on the line against the Obsidian Nightmare, Corvus. Ware: May the French die trying! Gibson: Jadian Bridden squaring off with Mayhem in the first of two, count 'em, two cage matches. Blood Angel and "Peerless" Hunter Sabuani, one on one, albeit with the constant threat of the Dead Man's Hand. Can Edward Lambert overcome the odds? Ware: No he can't! Gibson: The Livewire Champion Elliot "Fucking" Rollins puts his gold on the line. The True Prophet and The Demon Vassago would love to snatch that from his grasp. The Strength in Numbers titles will be at stake, along with the very career of Christian Darke. This is one of the best cards I've seen, we are out of time to explain it all. "RAHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHH!" The camera pans the ringside falling on Jason Myers standing in the center of the ring.. Gibson: What a way to kick of the evening with our Gateway title defense. Ware: And you don't have to sit through a day long pre-show! Gibson: Joaquin Pierre defends his Sin City Championship Wrestling Gateway Championship against the reigning AWC LiveWire Champion, Corvus. Ware: I wonder if the winner will call himself the Sin City Championship Wrestling Club GatewayWire Champion? Gibson: Both Corvus and Pierre entered SCCW at the same time. Corvus has had the better record, including a title win over him at FUSE on ESPN. Pierre however has had better fame since joining, by capturing the Gateway Championship from El Diablo on the very last edition of Uproar. Ware: Which was also the last time we saw El Diablo in action, before the border patrol got a hold of him. Gibson: So without further ado, let's get our first match underway! The air within the arena feels like it has dropped several degrees colder as the house lights dim to the point where the whole building is pitch black. Jason Myers stands in the ring with his introduction cards and lifts the microphone. Myers: Making his way to the ring from Las Vegas, Nevada by way of Parts Unknown. He weighs in at 288 lbs and stands 6 foot 6. Accompanied by Lyndsay, He is known as the "Obsidian Nightmare", this is CORRRRRRRRRRRRVUSSSSSSSSSSS! As soon as his name is announced the fast paced opening of "Smiling Like A Killer" by Motorhead cranks out through the arena's sound system and the devious Duke of Deception pushes through the curtain and starts to walk a few steps before he stops dead in his tracks to look at all the fans that are booing him on either side of the aisle. Behind him stands the former lawyer of Scott Taylor, Lyndsay Bougenvilla. Gibson: This man is absolutely devastating in the ring, Eugene, we've seen him put people down and out with that Chapter 13 finishing move of his time after time, and he has all the tools to be a dominant force for a long time to come. Ware: Plus, you know, Pierre is French. Thus he is contractually obligated to be gay and lose fights. I'm the one you never see, in the dead of night, Peeking in your window, staying out of sight, Go to bed, lock the door, don't look in the mirror, What if I was right behind you, Smiling Like A Killer I'm the knocking at your door, when you're all alone, I'm the scratching zombie claw, in your twilight zone, Cut your throat, catch your breath, blood run like a river, Last thing you see before your death, Smiling Like A Killer, Killers in the house, Killers in the yard, Smile, smile, smile, Killers they're so hard, Smiling at your kids, Smiling at your mom, Smiling at your pets, They know where you come from, They know where you come from Gibson: The Atlantic Wrestling Club Livewire Champion is in the house, but will he be wearing that strap at the end of the night? Ware: He'll be wearing that and the Gateway Championship. Corvus conceitedly walks to the ring moving his arms out of the way if any of the fans reach out to try and touch him no matter if they are a fan or not. He walks around to the hard camera side and pulls himself up onto the apron and stands directly in the middle while he's leaning on the ropes. Lyndsay trails from behind I'm the razor at your throat, in your fever dreams, I'm the one to get your goat, nothing's what it seems, Freeze your smile, stop your heart, creep and crawl and slither, Come to you and make your mark, Smiling Like A Killer, Killers in your room, Squirming in your chair, Smile, smile, smile, Killers they're not fair, Smiling in the crowd, Smiling all alone, Smiling in your ear, Call you on the phone, Call you on the phone Corvus pulls out a flask from the back of his pants and brings it to his lips. He tosses the flask aside and looks down at the row of fans in front of him who are talking trash. The Obsidian Nightmare gives them an "up yours" gesture before he leans back extending his arms in a crucifix pose and spits out a reddish liquid into the air which sprays some of the fans, Lyndsay and also himself. Red droplets run down his face as he smirks and turns slightly to step inside the ring. Gibson: I wonder what that red stuff really is. Ware: We don't want to know, Dave. It's best not to ask questions... at least sometimes. I'm the creepy homeless guy, hanging around your school, I'm the one to make you gone, low and base and cruel, In the evening we can dance, piggy in the middle, I will show you true romance, Smiling Like A Killer, Killers in the mist, Killers in the fog, Smile, smile, smile, They might eat your dog, Smiling here and now, Smiling all the time, Smiling in your dreams Lyndsay remains on the outside of the ring, roughly following Corvus movements from outside. Corvus walks across the ring and climbs up the turnbuckle and points to himself and the camera gets close enough to him that his lips can be read. He is telling the fans that he's the ultimate competitor in FUSE and that they should all get on their knees and bow to him. He drops back down to the mat and turns to be inspected for foreign objects by the official but he comes up clean; then Corvus moves to the ropes and uses them to stretch. Gibson: The Obsidian Nightmare looks as ready as I've seen him, and this is going to be an all out war. Ware: As much as I hate him, Pierre can take punishment and come back for more like no Frenchman I've ever seen. And these two guys absolutely hate each other. War may be too pretty a term for this one. All lighting in the arena dims drastically, only the barest glow around the staging area as the sound of a guitar is head and the SinScreen comes to life. A cross appears on the screen, made of two swords, and on each blade is a name. Joaquin on the vertical cross, and Pierre on the horizontal. At this, the crowd goes nuts as "Cold (But I'm Still Here)" by Evans Blue hits the speakers. Hello, I'm your martyr Will you be my gangster? Can you feel my trigger hand moving further down your neck? When you hide, just hide inside that body But just remember that when I hold you The more you shake, the more you give away. Just before the chorus hits, a single bright spotlight shines down on the stage, illuminating the form of Joaquin Pierre who stands with his head bowed as if he is praying. He wears black hooded sweatshirt with the hood up, and in his right hand is his ever-present cane sword. The SCCW Gateway Championship gleams at Pierre's waist. Across his left shoulder rides the double-sided flag that flies both the French tricolor and the Stars and Stripes of the United States. COLD, but I'm still here BLIND, cause I'm so blind, say never We're far from comfortable this time. COLD, now we're so cold MINE, and your not mine, say never We're far from obvious this time. Pierre slowly raises his head and pulls down the hood of his sweatshirt, allowing his cobalt blue eyes to sweep the cheering fans before locking on the ring before him. He begins a slow walk to the ring, acknowledging the fans good natured cheering of Old Glory and booing the French tricolor, but not taking his eyes off of the ring. As he reaches the halfway point of coming down the ramp, the big figure of The True Saint exits from backstage. The dim lighting glints on his white leather outfit, and he quickly joins Pierre at the bottom of the ramp. Wait, another minute here. Time will kill us after all. Can you feel its second hand? Wrapped around your neck. So fall into my eyes, and fall into my lies. But don't you forget, the more you turn away, the more I want you to stay... COLD, but I'm still here BLIND, cause I'm so blind, say never We're far from comfortable this time COLD, now we're so cold. MINE, and your not mine, say never We're far from obvious this time. As the second verse ends, Pierre makes a complete circle around the ring, stopping to hand his sword and flag to the timekeeper to hold onto. As he finishes the circle, he does a vertical leap, landing on his feet on the ring apron, and then again jumping over the top rope. You're so endearing, you're so beautiful Well I don't look like they do, and I don't love like they do But I don't hate like they do Am I ever on your mind? COLD, but I'm still here. BLIND, Cause I'm so blind, say never We're far from comfortable this time. COLD, now we're so cold. MINE, and your not mine, say never We're far from obvious this time. He walks to each corner of the ring, at each corner pounding his chest with a fist three times before throwing the fist into the air and bringing the crowd to their feet. As the song reaches its final verse, he moves right to the center of the ring, bowing his head and closing his eyes. He pulls the sweatshirt off over his head and tosses it into the crowd, handing the Gateway Championship to Jason Myers for safekeeping. COLD, you broke me from the very first night. I'll love you till they day I die. I'm far too comfortable this time. COLD, I loved you from the very first night. You'll break me till the day I die I'm far too obvious this time. Pierre raises his eyes, locking them on his opponent, and throws shadow punches and kicks, warming himself up for the upcoming battle. Gibson: We just talked about Corvus, but no doubt in my mind, Pierre is ready for this. Ware: Intensity is a tool that can only get him so far, Dave. He's gonna need to actually not pussy out in this one. Pierre adjusts his foot wrap while waiting for the bell. Corvus, like a caged animal stands, his ankles tense and ready to pounce. Referee Adam Jacobs signals for the bell, and the time keeper obliges. *DING* *DING* *DING* Gibson: Corvus pounces into action taking Pierre to the canvas with a double leg takedown as soon as this match gets underway. Corvus rolls around Pierre hooking him into a front face lock. Pierre places his hands against the forearm of Corvus and tries to push the arm away but Corvus is too powerful for him. Gibson: Corvus has a front face lock applied, he's looking to wear Pierre down early in the match, and then just pick him apart later. Ware: Good strategy. Corvus yanks Pierre up with him tightening his forearm as he does so. Jacobs doesn't notice the blatant choke for a moment and before he can Corvus drives a knee into the ribcage of the Gateway Champion. Gibson: A series of knees to the French Champion. WHAT A NECKBREAKER! Ware: Corvus snapping necks! Corvus sits up from the canvas with a grin across his face. He rolls to a knee and grabs Pierre by the head applying an inverted face lock on him. Corvus pulls his knee off of the canvas and yanks Pierre up into a standing inverted face lock. A quick motion drives Pierre's neck down across the Obsidian Nightmare's right knee. Corvus grabs the arm and holds the battered French warrior across his knee in a modified dragon sleeper. Gibson: Brutal attack here early and if Joaquin Pierre can't counter this somehow, there's no way he'll escape this match as Champion. Ware: Could you have said something more obvious there? Really, Dave, use your head. Pierre struggles uselessly against the hold, until Corvus yanks him back to a vertical base. The Gateway Champion however has no chance at escaping before the AWC LiveWire Champion takes him to the canvas swiftly with a dragon sleeper DDT. Gibson: Devastating reverse DDT from Corvus, and now the pinfall! Ware: The reign of the Obsidian Nightmare begins! Jacobs makes the count. 1... 2... Kick out! Gibson: Our Gateway Champion will not go down without a fight, Corvus is going to need to throw a lot more at him if he plans to win this match. Ware: Pierre is committing treason! Treason I say! No French man is supposed to put up a fight, he's supposed to quit before he even gets started! An aggravated Corvus yanks Pierre back to his feet, hooks his arms behind the back and with a quick pop of his hips sends Pierre to the canvas with an overhead belly to belly suplex. With momentum still on his side he hits the ropes, leaps into the air and drives both knees square into the face of Pierre. Gibson: Corvus dominating here, double knees to the face and Pierre will be lucky if he doesn't have a broken nose. Ware: DAMNATION! That had to hurt big time. Cockily Corvus yanks Pierre to his feet in a front face lock, Pierre's body drapes limply in Corvus' arms. The Obsidian Nightmare tosses the Gateway Champion onto his shoulders, and shouts "Bankrupt!". Gibson: Chapter 13! Ware: Corvus beats Pierre via FUCKING KILLING HIM! Suddenly Pierre slips out grabbing Corvus head as he falls to the canvas, bringing the face of his foe into both of his knees. Corvus' face bounces off Pierre's knees and lands onto the canvas. Gibson: And the momentum shifts here, Pierre countered out of desperation, showing his true heart, his resiliency, his will to win! Ware: It wont last! The French government is on its way here to arrest him for fighting too hard! Pierre rolls onto his stomach, neither man moves. 1... 2... Ware: Stay down! 3... Pierre begins to stir, Corvus is starting to stir as well. 4... 5... Pierre gingerly gets to his feet, pivots and walks right into a right hand from Corvus, BUT Pierre blocks it, and sends a right hand of his own. In the broadcast booth, Eugene Ware is frantically dialing numbers into his cellphone. Gibson: Blocked and this battle rages on! Ware: How is this happening? I'm losing money every minute this matches goes on! Corvus tries again and then again but all end up with the same result. Corvus obviously annoyed rakes the eyes of "Frenchie". Pierre leans against the ropes trying to regain his vision and catch his breath but, Corvus kicks his legs out from under him. Ware: Hello, I'd like to change my bet on the Pierre/Corvus match... Corvus still to win, but... what the HELL do you mean you can't change the bet now? This is a ripoff! Pierre clutches his right knee, rolling on the canvas in pain. Corvus wraps it up in his legs and drops a knee across it. Pierre hollers out in pain. Gibson: Corvus seemingly has the answer to everything! Ware: I demand a refund! Corvus then applies a spinning toehold, crosses Pierre's legs and kneels on them. Corvus applies the pressure pushing Pierre to the brink of submission before. Gibson: The submission could be coming here, Pierre looks like he's this close to giving it up here. Ware: They said that about my prom date too, and it never happened! Corvus releases the hold, a sick smile across his face. He scoops Pierre up and slams him into the turnbuckle with the tree of woe. Corvus walks to the other side of the ring smiles, runs and tries to deliver a running front dropkick... Gibson: PIERRE PULLS HIMSELF UP AND CORVUS FINDS HIMSELF IN A PAINFUL SITUATION! Ware: That hurt my balls! Pierre loosens his legs and straddles the top. Corvus pulls himself along the mat clutching his balls. Pierre looks behind him, Corvus starts to get up. Pierre moonsaults back and hits a moonsault cross body that takes Corvus to the mat. Pierre hooks the leg. Gibson: Tremendous athleticism there! Ware: Pussy... 1...   2...   Thre... No Corvus kicks out in the knick of time. Gibson: The AWC Livewire Champion not done just yet, Eugene. Pierre is going to need to bring more to the table than that. Pierre rolls and gets to his feet watching as Corvus gets to his. Pierre leaps up and catches him with a high knee, rolling through and getting right back to his feet. The fans pop. "RAHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHH!" Pierre springboards off middle rope and catches Corvus with a kick to the side of the head. Gibson: Stiff kick there! That was a thing of beauty! Ware: DAMN IT! Pierre kips up. Pierre holds the top rope, leaps to the top and pushes off moonsaulting and delivering a double knee to the face of Corvus. Pierre wraps the arm, drops the knee and hooks an armlock elevated Texas cloverleaf. Corvus struggles in pain, he slowly inches his way to the ropes and grasps the bottom rope. Referee Jacobs breaks hold. Pierre gets Corvus up in front face lock and hoists him up for a snap suplex. Pierre rolls backwards to feet, hits the ropes, he somersaults forward into a handstand, he pushes off and drops a leg across the throat of Corvus. Gibson: Corvus trying to get air after that blow, Pierre has made a definite mark on The Obsidian Nightmare here, I think Corvus may have underestimated him here. Ware: Nah, he's just letting him get some offense in to make him feel better. Pierre hoists Corvus to his feet. Corvus attacks the midsection with three hard rights followed up by a European uppercut that sends Pierre into the corner. Corvus charges with a high knee in the corner, hooks him and delivers a hellacious bulldog into the center of the ring. Ware: See? Corvus has very clearly decided that Pierre's playtime is over, it's time to get killed in the face now. Corvus grabs Pierre's legs wrapping them around his waist, and lifts him up holding him under his armpits. Corvus slams Pierre's face into turnbuckle, pivots and drops him to the canvas with a wheelbarrow sitout facebuster. Corvus moves Pierre's feet over his shoulders, rolls to side and hooks Pierre in a modified victory roll. Gibson: Pinfall! Ware: This is it! 1... 2... Thre...NO! Kick out by Pierre Corvus obviously aggitated slaps the canvas as Pierre begins to get to his feet. Corvus grabs him, hoists him and delivers a split legged spinebuster. Corvus holds the legs and tries to roll Pierre to his stomach but Pierre fights him all the way. Ware: Wow, he is a scrappy bastard, isn't he? Gibson: He's the kind of Gateway Champion we can be proud of, whether or not he wins this thing. Finally Corvus rolls the struggling Pierre onto his stomach for an elevated boston crab. Pierre creeps towards the ropes, Lyndsay hops up onto the ring apron and begins to step into the ring, Jacobs intercepts and argues with her just as Pierre grabs the bottom rope. Corvus yanks him away and into the center of the ring. "BOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOO!" Gibson: As tough as he is, this may be very close to the end, Ware: Thank god, I can still get my money back. Suddenly True Saint runs around the ring and Lyndsay hops off the apron. "RAHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHH!" Gibson: The True Saint just sent Lyndsay scampering for cover, but Pierre is still trapped in that hold. Ware: TAP OUT! Jacobs returns his attention to the action inside the ring and asks Pierre if he wants to give up. Gibson: Pierre fighting with everything he has here. Pierre refuses to give up, slowly crawling towards the ropes centimeter by centimeter. Gibson: PIERRE'S GOT THE ROPES! CORVUS WON'T LET GO! Corvus releases the hold by the count of four and starts stomping on Pierre's back. Jacobs forces him away and Lyndsay gets up pulling Pierre's neck across the bottom rope and stepping on it. "BOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOO!" Gibson: Now that is uncalled for! Corvus has had this match in his favor nearly the entire time! Ware: What the hell is going on here? "RAHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHH!" Gibson: Here comes the Gateway Champion's equalizer! Ware: Get your hands off her, you gorilla! The True Saint yanks her off the apron catching her and carrying her to the back kicking and screaming. OK not quite a million but I've got three months pay on Corvus, damn it! Jacobs turns to see the commotion and Corvus yanks Pierre to his feet. Chop to the thigh, another chop to the thigh. Kick and another kick followed up with a spinning back fist. Pierre stumbles away, Corvus grabs him and hoists him onto his shoulders. Out of instinct Pierre rolls through and rolls up the AWC LiveWire Champ with a schoolboy. Gibson: Rollup! If Pierre gets him here... Ware: Covus, he said something about your mama! Kill him! 1... 2... Kick out! Gibson: So close! Damn it, that was close! Ware: The dream lives on, David! Both explode to their feet and Corvus nearly beheads Pierre. Pierre rolls away from Corvus hardly able to believe what just happened. Jason Myers enters the ring, as Adam Jacobs hoists Pierre's arm high into the air. Corvus rolls out of the ring pointing to the True Saint as he backs up the ramp. Gibson: Spectacular effort here, Joaquin Pierre just proved to the world that he is no pushover, and that no matter what, this guy is for real. Ware: No hookers for three months... what am I supposed to do? ---- Who Knew SCCW Would Supply Free Headaches? There is a man standing in the backstage area of Sin City Championship Wrestling. If you look at the man he is neither good looking or ugly; just a plain man with a shaved head, a light brown Van Dyke beard on his face, which is busy and somewhat unkempt, has a dark five o’clock shadow, and has light brown eyes. He is only thirty years old but with the facial hair he could pass as someone in his forties. The man is wearing a pair of denim Judo shorts, a black tank top, and black work boots. He has a plain gold Timex watch on his wrist and a black vinyl glove on his misshapen left hand. The man looks around thoughtfully wondering where he should go. Should he meet some of the roster members? Should he get acquainted with the backstage personnel? Or should he tell Smitty T. Duluth that he has arrived earlier than expected? He hasn’t officially signed a contract with the company yet, and he wants to get the ball rolling as soon as possible. For now he should find Duluth’s office and get a contract signed. He walks forward, still looking around thoughtfully. His old habits have remained in Japan. Normally he could find his way around in the backstage of any Japanese wrestling venue. He has spent most of his career in Japan and hasn’t wrestled much in the United States. So you can say he’s a Gaijin to the United States. Someone is trying to get his attention. A jolly, portly man, weighing at least three hundred pounds, is running towards him. He can hear how grossly out of shape this man is, and it remains him when he was in the three hundred pound ballpark. Luckily for the man he has already lost over thirty pounds and he’s looking to lose about fifty more to really get into the shape he desires. Ethan Peters: You there, sir! Can I have a word with you? The man has stopped where he is, waiting for the portly fellow to catch up. Finally Ethan Peters, one of SCCW’s many interviewers, stops about a couple of feet away from the unknown man moving about in the backstage area. Ethan Peters: Hoo! I need to shed some meat off my bones. Unknown Man: I know how you feel sir. I used to weigh over three hundred myself. Ethan Peters: Are you one of the--- Ethan is trying to catch his breath. He is having a hell of a time but he’s managing as best as he can. Ethan Peters: Are you one of the newest members to the SCCW? Unknown Man: Ayuh I am. My name is Upton Osgood. Upton extends his right hand to Ethan Peters. He shakes it fiercely. Good handshake; Upton likes that. What Upton doesn’t like however, is that Peters is ogling at his left hand. Ethan Peters: What’s up with your hand, Upton? Upton remains silent for a moment trying not to let this overweight reporter get the best of him. Ever since he was a kid, Upton would get viciously teased by his peers and by those who were much older than him, calling him names like "Finger Freak," "Upchuck" and "Two Ton." Now he’s thirty years old and he hear those tormenting voices again. Instead of exploding on Ethan he takes a deep breath, slowly lets it out, and looks at the pudgy reporter. Upton Osgood: Is there something wrong with it, sir? Ethan Peters: Well yeah, I mean look at it! It looks like you have a penis for a hand! A penis for a hand. That’s a new one. When you hold a pay-per-view event in a makeshift, open-air arena at the end of an airplane runway on a secure military base, there are some questions you have to answer. Where are the fans going to sit? Where will they pee? Where will your wrestlers get changed? That type of thing is one of the reasons that more wrestling shows aren't held outside the friendly confines of a sports arena, where all those decisions are pretty much predetermined. It's the locker room question that becomes immediately pressing here for one of SCCW's guests. It's not the ritziest locker room in the history of the sport -- just an area pressed up against the side of the hangar that looms over the arena like an overeager stage parent. Because this is the United States Marine Corps, there are two tents, covered in tarps to prevent nosy photographers in helicopters from getting a sneak peek they can sell to a sleazy dirtrag. Ware: Oh, the perfectmanity! ---- Some Alone Time The locker room area of a professional wrestling event is hardly the first place that comes to mind when one thinks of "glamour". Row upon row of identical grey metal boxes, padlocks dangling from each. The near constant sound of the shower from off in the distance. The air redolent with sweat, moisture and the faint tang of blood. Truth be told, most wrestlers like spending as little time in the locker room as possible. Why stay in the back, after all, when all the drama is happening out front? So they step on in, take as much time as needed to change into whatever rather large men wear when they go out to beat the crap out of other large men and head right back out again. This, if you're a wrestler in a particular frame of mind, actually makes the locker room the perfect place to be. The relative solitude allows one the time to clear their heads, gather their thoughts, steel themselves for the battle ahead. Getting into the right head space, after all, can be crucial. For the couple we're about to meet, it's absolutely essential. Lance and Alanna Marshall had come to the arena this evening believing that they were simply going to serve as ringside muscle for the match between Jadian Bridden and Mayhem. Due to an announcement by Smitty T Duluth earlier in the card, however, their original plans have been rather thoroughly laid to waste. For what Smitty had to say was rather a surprise. Whether it was a good surprise or a bad surprise has yet to be decided. Lance is seated on one of the long wooden benches common to these sorts of rooms, a black iPod and a pair of earbuds off to his right side. He looks about half dressed for work, pairing his trademark lion's head wrestling trunks and boots with a black T-shirt. We can't see his face at the moment but, from the set of his shoulders, Lance Marshall does not appear to be particularly bothered by anything at the moment. The same cannot be said for his wife, manager and all-around support system, Alanna. She, too, is clad in a back T-shirt, this one reading "Irresistible Force" in white lettering across the chest. Her black jeans, as usual, look spray painted on and taper gracefully down her leg to her feet where she's currently sporting a pair of black Reeboks. In a very athletic sort of way, her look could easily be considered rather sexy. The effect is ruined, however, by the look of concern on Alanna's face. Her foot is tapping against the floor a mile a minute, beating out what sound like a nervous energy fueled Morse code. The nails on her right hand are running a counter rhythm, tapping against one of the lockers. This continues on for the briefest of intervals before we hear Lance speak. Lance: Baby, you know I love you...but please stop that. You're driving me crazy. Alanna, catching herself, has to force herself to tamp down the nervous energy. Not entirely successful, she instead redirects it into pacing around the room. Alanna: I don't like this. Not one damn bit. Something smells about this whole thing. Lance: This whole thing reeks, let's be honest...but it's not like we haven't had to deal with this kind of thing before. Fed heads pull this kind of thing all the time. They want to try and reward somebody, they want to try and screw somebody over...for all we know, Smitty's doing this to fuck with Mayhem, not us. Lance now turns around to face Alanna. Oddly, for someone about to walk into what Lance is about to walk into, his expression is oddly calm. Lance: So, relax, okay. We'll get through this like we got through everything else we've had to deal with over the years. Hey, with you by my side, babe, how can I fail? Lance holds out his hand to Alanna, beckoning her to sit down beside him. She walks over, sitting down beside him and resting herself against him. Alanna: I'm just worried, that's all. Lance: I'll be nice to Jadian, I promise. Despite herself, Alanna has to laugh. Alanna: Not that, you jackass. I mean...we haven't approached this week like this was a match. We were just supposed to be down at ringside keeping Mayhem's band of goons from mixing it up with the raving bitch Jadian seems to hitched his wagon to. And now...now we're smack dab in the middle of things. Lance reaches down, gingerly cradling her face in his right hand. Lance: Hey...if you're about to say "I don't know if we're ready", then don't. All that's changed is that now we know we're gonna be mixing it up instead of thinking we might be. And mixing it up is what we do, y'know? So, we're covered. We're cool. We are so money it hurts. A laugh from Alanna. Alanna: Swingers?! Lance, to his credit, manages to look mildly abashed. Lance: Best I could do off the top of my head. Sorry. Before Alanna can respond, Lance bends his head down and meets her mouth with a kiss. It is a kiss not of lust or inflamed passions but one of deep and abiding love. After a few moments, the two break. Alanna stares at her husband with a mixture of wonder and respect. Alanna: Thank you, baby. Lance: No problem. Do me a favor? Alanna: Sure. Lance: I gotta get my head right. Mind getting me a bottle of water? Alanna: No problem. Alanna gets up, striding forcefully forward towards the locker room door. When she gets about halfway, she stops and turns back to Lance. Alanna: I love you. Lance: I love you too, babe. And from the looks on both of their faces, there is no surer thing in the world. Alanna turns back towards the door, continuing her walk forward and out of camera shot. Once he hears the door close behind her with a thump, Lance reaches over and grabs the iPod. Sliding a bud into each ear, he clicks the device on and then cycles through the menus until he finally finds what it is he's looking for. It's impossible to clearly make out what Lance is listening to. But two things can be gleaned by even a casual, brief listen. The song is a driving, propulsive beast of a thing. And it is loud. ---- Vassago vs The True Prophet vs Elliott Rollins Myers: The following is a Triple Threat match and it is for the Sin City Championship Wrestling Livewire championship! Ware: Jono doesn't have it in him! I knew it! He's taking the coward's win! Gibson: Thank God for that! In a business filled with the likes of, no offense, Mayhem, Wyatt Connors and Alexandra Pierce, the likes of Jonathan Rhine is a breath of refreshing air! Misery: Amazing.....absolutely.....amazing. As Jonathan Rhine slowly and unsteadily makes his way up the corner of the cage, the camera shifts to the area at the top of the ramp, where Misfit and Mayhem have been watching the match. Mayhem stands, his arms crossed over his taped ribs, impassively watching. Misfit, on the other hand, is standing, and chanting "RHINE!!! RHINE!!! RHINE!!!" along with the rest of the crowd. Perhaps Eugene Ware is right, and speaking Desade's real name draws her attention, summoning her, because she just walked through the curtain to stand at the top of the ramp. She turns her head, and she and Mayhem stare at each other a moment before she simply nods to him, and after a moment, he returns the nod, and they both resume watching the match, Desade's hand on her chin, her index finger tapping her lips thoughtfully. Gibson: Jonathan Rhine slowly climbing up the cage, looking to get over and onto the ground, securing his victory without being forced to inflict any more punishment on Aimz! Misery: He's not going to make it. Ware: Wha? Misery: Campbell is getting up. She'll catch him. It looks like Misery is right, because Aimz is up and on her feet, already scaling the turnbuckles to grasp at Rhine's feet. The camera catches their dialog as he shakes his leg, attempting to dislodge her. Rhine: Let go, Amy! Just....Let it go! Aimz: It doesn't end like this, Jon! You can't fucking run from this! Get your ass down here and FACE ME! He kicks at her, but it is halfhearted, and when she spills off him, she falls back to a seated position, quickly getting back to her feet. Back at the top of the ramp, Desade merely shakes her head, consults her pocketwatch and steps over to Mayhem. He lowers his head, and she whispers something to him, to which he nods thoughtfully as she walks through the curtain and backstage. Gibson: Aimz back on her feet, climbing again to stop Rhine! Ware: Am....Am I the only one watching the monitors? Did you guys just see..... Misery: See what? Ware: Nothing. Nevermind. This time, Aimz waits until she is further up before she grabs at Rhine, but he squirms and climbs a few feet further, out of her grasp. She counters by turning, putting her back in the corner, wedging herself between the cage and Rhine, peeling him off so he is sitting on her shoulders. She pushes away from the cage, at the last second, spinning, pirouetting both of them 180 degrees... Ware: Whoa! Isn't that.... Misery: Total Mayhem! Campbell just slammed Jonathan Rhine's head off the turnbuckles with a top rope Total Mayhem! AT&T Replay Aimz positions herself so Rhine is sitting on her shoulders, facing her. With a hard push using the cage for leverage, she prys him off the fence, and for a moment it looks like she is simply going to powerbomb him off the top ropes. Rhine flails, trying to grab the fence, and for just a moment, his last two fingers twine through. But this gives Aimz the leverage to pivot, throwing herself backwards off the top rope, dragging Rhine down as she sits, slamming his head against the top and bottom turnbuckle on the way down. Gibson: Amy Campbell has pulled Jon Rhine into the middle of the ring, and hooks a leg as she goes for a pin! ONE!   TWO!!   THRE- "RAHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHH!" Misery: Campbell can't believe it! Ware: I don't believe it! Gibson: Jonathan Rhine just reached down and from God only knows where, summoned up enough energy to get his shoulder up! Misery: Both wrestlers laying, spent, on their backs in the middle of the ring! And Oz does his duty as a referee, and begins to count. ONE!!!       TWO!!!       THREE!!! Gibson: Aimz on her feet, wobbles her way over to the cage wall and begins to climb! Rhine still flat on his back! Ware: Is it me, or does that cage wall seem about as steady as Aimz? The preternaturally observant Eugene Ware is right again, and the higher Aimz climbs, the more the cage wall wobbles. Misery: When the cage fell, it must have broken the supports in that corner. Amy's weight is more than the structure can support. Ware: Are you saying she's fat? OW!!! Gibson: Aimz nearly three-quarters of the way up the cage now, but Rhine is quickly back on his feet again, and he charges at Aimz, jumping up and...FUCK! David Gibson rarely swears, and he usually gives his announce partner grief when Eugene does. So you can be assured that anything that makes Dave yell that particular word, it must be because he has a VERY good reason. When Jonathan Rhine charged the side of the cage that Aimz had nearly ascended, he jumped, clearing a good eight feet before slamming into the wall. The already unsteady cage wall. With a loud metal *PING*, the next set of supports down break free, and the entire cage wall sways outwards, threatening to dump the metal wall and both wrestlers across the first four or five rows on that side of the ring. However, the wall holds, and springs back, acting like a giant mesh catapult. Being lower, Jonathan Rhine has to deal with less of the whiplash and is only dumped from the cage wall. Unfortunately, his foot tangles in the top rope, and he is flipped backwards onto the back of his head and neck, rolling off to lay face down. Aimz is flung like a dart, from nearly fifteen feet above the ring, and all the way across it to smash into the ropes on the opposite side of the ring, dropping limply to the mat. "HOLY SHIT!!! HOLY SHIT!!! HOLY SHIT!!!" Gibson: EITHER ONE OF THEM COULD HAVE BEEN KILLED IN THAT FALL!! After a moment with neither wrestler moving, Oz starts his count. ONE!!!     TWO!!!     THREE!!!     FOUR!!!     FIVE!!! Gibson: Oz at the five count, halfway through, and Aimz is starting to stir! Misery: Rhine hasn't moved at all, but I think I can see him breathing. SIX!!!     SEVEN!!!     EIGHT!!! Ware: If you die, do you lose the title belt?   NIN- Gibson: AIMZ IS UP! AIMZ IS UP!! Aimz is indeed up, her legs wobbly, like a newborn giraffe, and as the camera zooms in on her face, her eyes keep threatening to roll into the back of her head. She turns, and slowly, hand over hand, starts to scale the cage wall. Gibson: My God, where is she getting it?!?! Amy Campbell on her feet after that brutal fall off a twenty foot steel cage, AND SHE'S CLIMBING!!! Ware: NOOOOO!!!! DON'T CLIMB!!!! GO PIN HIM!!! HE'S DEAD!!! NO CLIMBY AMY, NO CLIMBY!!! Misery: She's running on instinct, on autopilot. If that fall knocked her out, she may not even remember falling! She continues to scale, slowly, swooning on occasion like she is about to pass out and fall, but somehow, something in her takes over, and when those moments threaten, she wraps her fingers through the cage and clings. Ware: Rhine is moving! Rhine is standing! I TOLD YOU HE'S THE TERMINATOR!!! Gibson: Rhine making his way over to the wall under Aimz, and he looks up and begins climbing! Can he reach her before she makes it to the top!? And if he does, what is he going to do?? Aimz does reach the top of the cage before her opponent can reach her, mostly because he is climbing crookedly, at an angle that takes him away from her as he goes. She sways again, looking ready to fall, but heaves one leg over the top, sitting astraddle the cage. Unfortunately, that seems to be as far as her burst of energy will take her, and she totters, still threatening to fall. Ware: If she falls out, she hits the ground first, and wins, right? Gibson:Well, technically, yes, if she survived the fall that is.... Misery: Oh fuck this. There is a rustling sound, very much like a headset being hastily removed, and suddenly, Misery is at ringside, standing under Aimz, looking up expectantly at her. Ware: Is she....is she planning on catching her if she falls? Gibson: I believe that is exactly what she has in mind, Eugene! Ware: If she catches her and she doesn't touch the floor, does that mean she doesn't win? Gibson: I.....why in the name of God would you ask such an asinine question?! Ware: It's relevant! This is a Universal Title match! By now, Rhine has reached the top of the cage and straddles it, much like Amy does. He's ended up maybe five feet from her and he looks at her as she sways, her eyes still threatening to roll into the back of her head, and he begins scooting towards her. As he closes with her she looks at him with what looks like recognition, then she starts to slip backwards, falling out of the cage. Gibson: Jon Rhine just reached out and caught Amy Campbell! He just saved her from a career threatening, if not life threatening fall! Ware: If she fell, she hit first, and she won! He was protecting his belt! Rhine indeed caught Aimz just before she fell, and instead of holding her there, he pulls the both of them close together and wraps his arms around her in what could only be an embrace. He turns his head and whispers something in her ear, but her blood soaked hair obscures it enough that no lipreader in the world would be able to tell what he says. What happens next will be endlessly debated. Wrestling geeks will freeze frame and zoom and digitally enhance, and everyone will claim their determination is the right one. But ultimately, nobody but the two on top of the cage will ever know: Does whatever Jonathan Rhine says to Amy Campbell cause her to close her eyes and softly nod before she releases the cage? Or does she finally succumb to her injuries and fall unconscious? In the end, the result is the same. Suddenly, Rhine stands, pulling Amy up with him, and in one hard push, they are both airborne, arcing up, higher than even the top of the cage, illuminated by spotlights and the staccato bursts of flash bulbs. The entire audience is deathly silent, seeming to all inhale, gasping in unison... THHHHHWWWWAAAAAAAAAAAAACCCK! "HOLY SHIT!!! HOLY SHIT!!! HOLY SHIT!!!" Gibson: RHINE REWIND! RHINE REWIND FROM THE TOP OF A TWENTY FOOT STEEL CAGE!!! Ware: Rhine with the limp role over cover! For a moment, Oz just stands there, blinking at the two very still bodies in the ring. Then, suddenly, he remembers he's the one in the striped shirt, and drops down to count. ONE! TWO!!   THREE!!! *DING* *DING* *DING* "RAHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHH!" Gibson: Rhine wins! Rhine wins! But my God, at what cost!? In the ring, Oz has a microphone. Oz: Ladies and gentlemen! Your winner! "RAHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHH!" Oz reaches down and grabs Jonathan Rhine's hand, to lift it in victory. Oz: And still...Universal.... Oz trails off, letting Rhine's unresponsive hand fall, onto an equally unresponsive Aimz. Suddenly he cuts off the mic, and stands, motioning towards the ramp area frantically, and the camera in ring cuts away to a ringside camera, and dozens of paramedics swarming their way to the ring, but there seems to be a problem. Paramedic One: The cage door's wedged, I can't fucking get it open! Paramedic Two: Here, maybe if we both try... Misery: MOVE!! The Amazon doesn't wait for the paramedics to follow her barked order, shouldering them out of the way, planting her foot beside the door, gripping it in both hands and grunting. Finally, with a shout and the squeal of metal, the door rips free, spilling Misery backwards, the door clipping the camera blacking out the image for a moment. We switch to the top of the ramp camera, where numerous members of SCCW have spilled through the entry and are milling about. A short way down the ramp, Lance Marshall is struggling to hold Cyrus Raynes, who is desperately trying to get to the ring. Raynes: JON! AMY! JON!! Finally, Lance wraps his arms completely around Cyrus and lifts him, holding his feet off the floor as Lance's wife Alanna tries to calm the frantic Highwayman. Alanna: Cyrus! Cyrus! Calm, take it easy, let the paramedics do their job, you don't want to get in their way. The bald man stammers, looking ready to cry. Raynes: But...but....OK. We cut back to the announce table, where a very pale David Gibson sits facing the camera, and Eugene Ware stands on his chair trying to get a better view as the audience buzzes behind them. Gibson: Folks, I'm not 100% certain what is happening right now, the paramedics are seeing to both Jon Rhine and Amy Campbell in the ring after Rhine hit the Rhine Rewind off the top of the steel cage and for all appearances pinned her, retaining his Universal Title. Eugene Ware sits back down in his chair, his lips drawn into a frown. Ware: I take back every bad thing I ever said about Jon Rhine and Amy Campbell. Gibson: We're....we're out of time here....uh... Ware: Temptation, April 10th, Cinemax, uh....UCLA.   Fade to black.
